  1 | # Elith — GitHub Collaboration Strategy
  2 | **IBM Bob Hackathon | May 15–17, 2026**  
  3 | **For:** All 3 team members  
  4 | **Rule:** Commit often. Push constantly. Never hold work locally.
  5 | 
  6 | ---
  7 | 
  8 | ## Two Remotes
  9 | 
 10 | ```
 11 | main      → your personal GitHub repo (private, your backup only)
 12 | silo-hq   → team GitHub repo (everyone works here)
 13 | ```
 14 | 
 15 | **Basil Joy and Johann only ever see and use `silo-hq`.**  
 16 | `main` is yours alone — personal safety net.
 17 | 
 18 | ---
 19 | 
 20 | ## Remote Setup (You Do This — Hour 1)
 21 | 
 22 | ```bash
 23 | # 1. Create elith repo on silo-hq GitHub org
 24 | #    Go to github.com/Silo-HQ → New Repository
 25 | #    Name: elith
 26 | #    Private for now
 27 | #    No README
 28 | 
 29 | # 2. Clone silo-hq repo
 30 | git clone https://github.com/Silo-HQ/elith.git
 31 | cd elith
 32 | 
 33 | # 3. Add your personal remote
 34 | git remote add main https://github.com/Half-Silver/elith.git
 35 |  
 36 | # 4. Confirm both remotes are set
 37 | git remote -v
 38 | # Should show:
 39 | # origin   https://github.com/Silo-HQ/elith.git (fetch)
 40 | # origin   https://github.com/Silo-HQ/elith.git (push)
 41 | # main     https://github.com/Half-Silver/elith.git (fetch)
 42 | # main     https://github.com/Half-Silver/elith.git (push)
 43 | 
 44 | # 5. Create dev branch
 45 | git checkout -b dev
 46 | 
 47 | # 6. Create full folder structure
 48 | mkdir -p backend/router
 49 | mkdir -p backend/context_engine
 50 | mkdir -p backend/skills
 51 | mkdir -p backend/providers
 52 | mkdir -p backend/operations
 53 | mkdir -p backend/models
 54 | mkdir -p tui/screens
 55 | mkdir -p tui/components
 56 | mkdir -p frontend/src/components
 57 | mkdir -p frontend/src/pages
 58 | mkdir -p frontend/src/stores
 59 | mkdir -p obsidian-template/architecture
 60 | mkdir -p obsidian-template/standards
 61 | mkdir -p obsidian-template/tasks
 62 | mkdir -p bob-reports
 63 | mkdir -p docs
 64 | 
 65 | # 7. Create placeholder files
 66 | touch backend/__init__.py
 67 | touch backend/main.py
 68 | touch backend/router/__init__.py
 69 | touch backend/router/model_router.py
 70 | touch backend/context_engine/__init__.py
 71 | touch backend/context_engine/vault_reader.py
 72 | touch backend/context_engine/repo_scanner.py
 73 | touch backend/context_engine/packet_builder.py
 74 | touch backend/skills/__init__.py
 75 | touch backend/providers/__init__.py
 76 | touch backend/operations/__init__.py
 77 | touch bob-reports/.gitkeep
 78 | touch docs/.gitkeep
 79 | 
 80 | # 8. Create .gitignore
 81 | cat > .gitignore << 'EOF'
 82 | __pycache__/
 83 | *.pyc
 84 | *.pyo
 85 | .env
 86 | venv/
 87 | .venv/
 88 | node_modules/
 89 | .DS_Store
 90 | *.log
 91 | sessions/
 92 | tmp/
 93 | dist/
 94 | build/
 95 | .next/
 96 | EOF
 97 | 
 98 | # 9. Create .env.example
 99 | cat > .env.example << 'EOF'
100 | # IBM Bob
101 | BOB_ENABLED=true
102 | 
103 | # Anthropic
104 | ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=your_key_here
105 | 
106 | # Google
107 | GEMINI_API_KEY=your_key_here
108 | 
109 | # OpenAI
110 | OPENAI_API_KEY=your_key_here
111 | 
112 | # Ollama (local)
113 | OLLAMA_ENABLED=false
114 | OLLAMA_MODEL=llama3.1
115 | 
116 | # App
117 | REPO_PATH=~/projects/myapp
118 | VAULT_PATH=~/obsidian/myapp
119 | EOF
120 | 
121 | # 10. Create README placeholder
122 | cat > README.md << 'EOF'
123 | # Elith
124 | **Every model. Bob-level. Your choice.**
125 | 
126 | > Elith is a universal repo-aware agent framework that gives every AI model
127 | > the same codebase skills IBM Bob has natively.
128 | 
129 | *IBM Bob Hackathon 2026 — lablab.ai*
130 | EOF
131 | 
132 | # 11. Create MIT LICENSE
133 | cat > LICENSE << 'EOF'
134 | MIT License
135 | 
136 | Copyright (c) 2026 Silo HQ
137 | 
138 | Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy

144 | 
145 | The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in all
146 | copies or substantial portions of the Software.
147 | EOF
148 | 
149 | # 12. Push to BOTH remotes
150 | git add .
151 | git commit -m "init: project structure and placeholders"
152 | 
153 | git push origin dev          # → silo-hq (team sees this)
154 | git push main dev            # → your personal backup
155 | 
156 | # 13. Invite teammates on silo-hq
157 | #     Go to github.com/Silo-HQ/elith → Settings → Collaborators
158 | #     Add Basil Joy's GitHub username
159 | #     Add Johann's GitHub username
160 | ```
161 | 
162 | ---
163 | 
164 | ## First-Time Setup (Basil Joy + Johann)
165 | 
166 | **They only use silo-hq. They never touch your personal remote.**
167 | 
168 | ```bash
169 | # 1. Accept the GitHub invite from silo-hq (check email)
170 | 
171 | # 2. Clone from silo-hq
172 | git clone https://github.com/Silo-HQ/elith.git
173 | cd elith
174 | 
175 | # 3. Switch to dev
176 | git checkout dev
177 | git pull origin dev
178 | 
179 | # 4. Create your branch
180 | # Basil Joy:
181 | git checkout -b basil/ai-core
182 | git push origin basil/ai-core
183 | 
184 | # Johann:
185 | git checkout -b johann/ui
186 | git push origin johann/ui
187 | 
188 | # Done. Start building.
189 | ```
190 | 
191 | ---
192 | 
193 | ## Branch Map
194 | 
195 | ```
196 | silo-hq (origin)
197 | └── main                  ← demo-ready only. Hour 36 only.
198 | └── dev                   ← integration. Everyone merges here.
199 |     ├── you/backend        ← Team lead
200 |     ├── basil/ai-core      ← Basil Joy
201 |     └── johann/ui          ← Johann
202 | 
203 | your personal (main remote)
204 | └── dev                   ← your backup mirror
205 | └── main                  ← your backup mirror
206 | ```
207 | 
208 | ---
209 | 
210 | ## Your Push Routine (Team Lead Only)
211 | 
212 | You push to both remotes every time:
213 | 
214 | ```bash
215 | # Push to team + personal backup simultaneously
216 | git push origin [branch]     # → silo-hq
217 | git push main [branch]       # → your personal
218 | 
219 | # Or set up a push-to-both alias (run once):
220 | git config alias.pushall '!git push origin "$@" && git push main "$@"'
221 | 
222 | # Then just use:
223 | git pushall [branch]
224 | ```
225 | 
226 | ---
227 | 
228 | ## Basil Joy + Johann Push Routine
229 | 
230 | They only push to silo-hq (origin):
231 | 
232 | ```bash
233 | git add .
234 | git commit -m "wip: [what you did]"
235 | git push origin [your-branch]    # silo-hq only
236 | ```
237 | 
238 | ---
239 | 
240 | ## Folder Ownership
241 | 
242 | | Branch | Person | Folders They Own |
243 | |--------|--------|-----------------|
244 | | `you/backend` | Team Lead | `backend/main.py`, `backend/router/`, `backend/context_engine/`, `backend/operations/` |
245 | | `basil/ai-core` | Basil Joy | `backend/skills/`, `backend/providers/` |
246 | | `johann/ui` | Johann | `tui/`, `frontend/`, `docs/` |
247 | 
248 | **Never touch someone else's folder. Ever.**
249 | 
250 | ---
251 | 
252 | ## Commit Every 2 Hours (Everyone)
253 | 
254 | ```bash
255 | git add .
256 | git commit -m "wip: [what you just finished]"
257 | git push origin [your-branch]
258 | # Team lead also: git push main [your-branch]
259 | ```
260 | 
261 | ### Commit Message Format
262 | 
263 | ```bash
264 | git commit -m "init: claude provider skeleton"
265 | git commit -m "wip: read_file skill half done"
266 | git commit -m "feat: read_file skill complete and tested"
267 | git commit -m "fix: search_code grep path error"
268 | git commit -m "done: all 12 skills complete"
269 | ```
270 | 
271 | ---
272 | 
273 | ## Sync Merge (Hours 9, 17, 25, 33, 41)
274 | 
275 | ### Step 1 — Everyone (Before Sync Call)
276 | ```bash
277 | git checkout dev
278 | git pull origin dev
279 | git checkout [your-branch]
280 | git merge dev
281 | # Fix conflicts if any → message team lead
282 | git push origin [your-branch]
283 | ```
284 | 
285 | ### Step 2 — Team Lead (During Sync Call)
286 | ```bash
287 | git checkout dev
288 | git pull origin dev
289 | 
290 | git merge basil/ai-core --no-ff -m "merge: basil ai-core → dev"
291 | git merge johann/ui --no-ff -m "merge: johann ui → dev"
292 | git merge you/backend --no-ff -m "merge: backend → dev"
293 | 
294 | git push origin dev      # → silo-hq
295 | git push main dev        # → your personal backup
296 | ```
297 | 
298 | ### Step 3 — Everyone (After Sync Call)
299 | ```bash
300 | git checkout dev
301 | git pull origin dev
302 | git checkout [your-branch]
303 | git merge dev
304 | git push origin [your-branch]
305 | ```
306 | 
307 | ---
308 | 
309 | ## Integration Points
310 | 
311 | Only two files connect everyone's work. Team lead owns both.
312 | 
313 | **`backend/router/model_router.py`**  
314 | **`backend/main.py`**
315 | 
316 | **Basil Joy protocol:**  
317 | When a provider is done, message team lead:
318 | > "claude_provider.py done. Import: `from backend.providers.claude_provider import ClaudeProvider`"
319 | 
320 | **Johann protocol:**  
321 | When a new endpoint is needed, message team lead:
322 | > "Need GET /api/results/{session_id} returning { output, files_changed, report_path }"
323 | 
324 | ---
325 | 
326 | ## Hour 36 — Go Public + Final Merge
327 | 
328 | ```bash
329 | # Team lead only
330 | 
331 | # 1. Final merge into dev
332 | git checkout dev
333 | git pull origin dev
334 | git merge basil/ai-core --no-ff
335 | git merge johann/ui --no-ff
336 | git merge you/backend --no-ff
337 | git push origin dev
338 | git push main dev
339 | 
340 | # 2. Merge dev into main
341 | git checkout main
342 | git merge dev --no-ff -m "release: hackathon demo v1.0"
343 | git tag v1.0.0
344 | 
345 | # 3. Push to both remotes
346 | git push origin main --tags     # → silo-hq (submission URL)
347 | git push main main --tags       # → your personal backup
348 | 
349 | # 4. Make silo-hq/elith PUBLIC
350 | #    github.com/Silo-HQ/elith → Settings → Danger Zone → Make Public
351 | 
352 | # 5. Submission URL will be:
353 | #    https://github.com/Silo-HQ/elith
354 | ```
355 | 
356 | ---
357 | 
358 | ## Emergency Protocols
359 | 
360 | ### Broken code pushed to dev on silo-hq:
361 | ```bash
362 | # Team lead only
363 | git checkout dev
364 | git revert HEAD --no-edit
365 | git push origin dev
366 | git push main dev
367 | # Tell everyone to pull
368 | ```
369 | 
370 | ### Someone's laptop crashed:
371 | ```bash
372 | # They clone fresh from silo-hq
373 | git clone https://github.com/Silo-HQ/elith.git
374 | cd elith
375 | git checkout [their-branch]
376 | # Last pushed commit is safe — continue from there
377 | ```
378 | 
379 | ### Merge conflict you can't solve:
380 | ```bash
381 | git merge --abort
382 | # Screenshot the conflict
383 | # Message team lead immediately
384 | ```
385 | 
386 | ---
387 | 
388 | ## Quick Reference Card (Screenshot This)
389 | 
390 | ```
391 | REMOTES:
392 |   origin = silo-hq (team repo — everyone)
393 |   main   = your personal (you only)
394 | 
395 | YOUR BRANCH:
396 |   Team Lead → you/backend
397 |   Basil Joy → basil/ai-core
398 |   Johann    → johann/ui
399 | 
400 | EVERY 2 HOURS:
401 |   git add .
402 |   git commit -m "wip: [what you did]"
403 |   git push origin [your-branch]
404 |   # Team lead also: git push main [your-branch]
405 | 
406 | BEFORE EVERY SYNC CALL:
407 |   git checkout dev && git pull origin dev
408 |   git checkout [your-branch] && git merge dev
409 |   git push origin [your-branch]
410 | 
411 | NEVER:
412 |   - Push directly to main branch
413 |   - Touch someone else's folder
414 |   - Sit on uncommitted work for more than 2 hours
415 |   - Merge into dev yourself (team lead only)
416 | ```
417 | 
418 | ---
419 | 
420 | *Elith GitHub Strategy — All Team Members — IBM Bob Hackathon 2026*

  1 | # Elith Backend Implementation Plan
  2 | **Your Role:** Architect + Team Lead  
  3 | **Current Status:** Starting Hours 2-4 - Backend Foundation
  4 | 
  5 | ---
  6 | 
  7 | ## Quick Reference
  8 | 
  9 | ### API Contract (Agreed with Johann)
 10 | ```
 11 | POST /api/scan       → Scan repo and vault
 12 | POST /api/execute    → Start operation, return session_id
 13 | GET  /api/stream/{session_id} → SSE stream of output
 14 | GET  /api/models     → Available/configured models
 15 | GET  /api/results/{session_id} → Session results
 16 | GET  /api/tasks      → Available operations
 17 | ```
 18 | 
 19 | ### Critical Dependencies
 20 | - **Basil Joy**: Provides all providers + skill layer
 21 | - **Johann**: Consumes your API for TUI + React frontend
 22 | - **You**: Backend orchestration, context engine, integration
 23 | 
 24 | ---
 25 | 
 26 | ## Project Structure
 27 | 
 28 | ```
 29 | elith/
 30 | ├── backend/
 31 | │   ├── main.py                    # FastAPI entry
 32 | │   ├── models/                    # Pydantic models
 33 | │   │   ├── task_packet.py
 34 | │   │   └── session.py
 35 | │   ├── routes/                    # API endpoints
 36 | │   │   ├── scan.py

 63 | ## Implementation Phases

 65 | ### HOURS 2-4: Backend Foundation ✅ CURRENT
 66 | **Goal:** FastAPI + repo scanner + vault reader + /api/scan working
 67 | 
 68 | **Tasks:**
 69 | 1. Create `pyproject.toml`, `requirements.txt`, `.env.example`, `.gitignore`
 70 | 2. Build Pydantic models (`task_packet.py`, `session.py`)
 71 | 3. Implement `repo_scanner.py` - walks directory, skips .git/node_modules
 72 | 4. Implement `vault_reader.py` - reads .md files from Obsidian vault
 73 | 5. Create `main.py` with FastAPI app + CORS
 74 | 6. Create `/api/scan` endpoint
 75 | 7. Test with curl
 76 | 
 77 | **Success Criteria:**
 78 | ```bash
 79 | curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/api/scan \

 81 |   -d '{"repo_path": "/path/to/repo"}'
 82 | # Returns: {files: [...], vault_notes: [...], total_files: N}

201 | ## Critical Path Risks

206 | | SSE streaming breaks on Railway | Test Railway SSE early, have fallback |
207 | | Novel arch output is generic | 6-8 hours prompt iteration (worth it) |
208 | | Johann's frontend can't connect | Check CORS settings first |
209 | 
210 | ---
211 | 
212 | ## Communication Protocol
213 | 
214 | - Sync call every 8 hours (Hours 1, 9, 17, 25, 33, 41)
215 | - Blocked? Message immediately - max 1 hour alone
216 | - Commit every 2 hours minimum
217 | - Update this plan as you discover issues
218 | 
219 | ---
220 | 
221 | ## Next Steps
222 | 
223 | You're starting **HOURS 2-4**. Begin with:
224 | 
225 | 1. Create project structure files (pyproject.toml, etc.)
226 | 2. Build Pydantic models
227 | 3. Implement repo_scanner.py
228 | 4. Implement vault_reader.py
229 | 5. Create FastAPI app with /api/scan
230 | 6. Test with curl
231 | 
232 | **Switch to Code mode when ready to implement.**
233 | 
234 | ---

  1 | # Elith — Your Tasks (Architect + Team Lead)
  2 | **IBM Bob Hackathon | May 15–17, 2026**  
  3 | **Your Role:** General — You design, wire, and integrate everything  
  4 | **Rule:** If anyone is blocked, they message you immediately. You unblock them within 30 minutes.
  5 | 
  6 | ---
  7 | 
  8 | ## Your Stack
  9 | - Python 3.10+
 10 | - FastAPI
 11 | - pathlib (context engine)
 12 | - subprocess (model runner coordination)
 13 | - SSE (Server-Sent Events for live streaming to frontend)
 14 | - Railway or Render (deployment)
 15 | 
 16 | ---
 17 | 
 18 | ## Hour-by-Hour Tasks
 19 | 
 20 | ---

 22 | ### Hour 1 — Team Sync (All 3 Together)

 31 |     GET  /api/models    → returns available configured models

 33 | ✓ Johann gets design reference (Bob Shell screenshot)
 34 | ✓ Basil Joy confirms Bob shell is installed
 35 | ✓ Split and go heads down

 38 | ---

 51 | ✓ Repo scanner

 55 |     └── skip: .git, node_modules, __pycache__, venv

 60 |     └── returns: list of notes with content + filename

 66 | Target: curl -X POST /api/scan -d '{"repo_path": "~/projects/test"}'
 67 |         returns clean JSON with file tree

 69 | 
 70 | ---
 71 | 
 72 | ### Hours 5–8 — Context Engine
 73 | ```
 74 | ✓ context_engine/packet_builder.py
 75 |     └── takes: repo scan + vault notes + task type
 76 |     └── scores file relevance for the task:
 77 |         - architect task → look for models, config, main files
 78 |         - test-gen task  → look for source files without test pairs
 79 |         - refactor task  → look for the target module + its imports
 80 |         - explain task   → look for entry points, README, main files
 81 |     └── returns top 4-6 files only

 87 | ✓ /api/execute endpoint STUB
 88 |     └── accepts: model, operation, repo_path, vault_path
 89 |     └── returns: session_id (UUID)
 90 |     └── returns mock streaming data for now
 91 |     └── (Basil Joy's providers plug in here later)
 92 | 
 93 | ✓ model_router.py skeleton
 94 |     └── empty provider slots ready for Basil Joy to fill
 95 | 
 96 | Target: Context engine picks the RIGHT 4-6 files for any task type
 97 |         /api/execute returns session_id and mock stream works

 99 | 
100 | ---
101 | 
102 | ### Hours 9–14 — Full Integration
103 | ```
104 | ✓ Wire model_router.py to Basil Joy's providers
105 |     └── import bob_provider, claude_provider, etc.
106 |     └── route based on model parameter

108 | ✓ /api/execute — real implementation
109 |     └── builds context packet

129 | ---

185 | 
186 | ---
187 | 
188 | ### Hours 29–36 — Demo Preparation
189 | ```
190 | ✓ Pick demo repo with Basil Joy:
191 |     Criteria:
192 |     - Real public GitHub repo (not a toy project)
193 |     - Medium size (50–200 files)
194 |     - Has some architectural debt worth improving

198 | ✓ Run full Elith workflow on demo repo:
199 |     - explain → capture output
200 |     - architect → capture the proposals (this is the demo moment)
201 |     - test-gen → show tests generated
202 | 
203 | ✓ Run same architect task on Bob → then Claude → show same quality
204 | 
205 | ✓ Ensure all Bob session reports are committed to repo

207 | ✓ README final version:
208 |     - What is Elith (2 sentences)
209 |     - How it works (3 bullet points)
210 |     - How to run locally
211 |     - How to configure models

214 | 
215 | ---
216 | 
217 | ### Hours 37–44 — Polish

222 |     └── requirements.txt final
223 |     └── pyproject.toml final
224 | 
225 | ✓ Make repo public (MIT license in place)

227 | ✓ Confirm deployment is stable
228 | ✓ Final README review

230 | 
231 | ---
232 | 
233 | ### Hours 45–47 — Submit
234 | ```
235 | ✓ GitHub repo public ← confirm
236 | ✓ Bob reports in repo ← confirm
237 | ✓ Video from Johann ← confirm
238 | ✓ Cover image from Johann ← confirm
239 | ✓ Fill submission form on lablab.ai:
240 |     └── Title: Elith
241 |     └── Short desc (255 chars): ready
242 |     └── Long desc (100+ words): ready
243 |     └── GitHub URL: ready
244 |     └── Demo URL: ready
245 | ✓ SUBMIT
246 | ```
247 | 
248 | ### Hour 48 — Buffer Only
249 | ```
250 | Do not build. Safety net for submission issues only.
251 | ```
252 | 
253 | ---
254 | 
255 | ## API Contract (Share With Johann)
256 | 
257 | ```
258 | Base URL: http://localhost:8000 (dev) / https://elith.up.railway.app (prod)
259 | 
260 | POST /api/scan
261 |   Body: { "repo_path": string, "vault_path": string? }
262 |   Returns: { files: FileInfo[], vault_notes: Note[], total_files: number }
263 | 
264 | POST /api/execute
265 |   Body: { "model": string, "operation": string, "repo_path": string, "vault_path": string? }
266 |   Returns: { "session_id": string }
267 | 
268 | GET /api/stream/{session_id}
269 |   Returns: SSE stream of { "type": "output"|"done"|"error", "content": string, "model": string }
270 | 
271 | GET /api/models
272 |   Returns: { "available": string[], "configured": string[] }
273 | 
274 | GET /api/results/{session_id}
275 |   Returns: { "output": string, "files_changed": string[], "report_path": string }
276 | ```
277 | 
278 | ---
279 | 
280 | ## Critical Path
281 | 
282 | These are YOUR blockers to watch:
283 | 
284 | | Risk | Your Action |
285 | |------|------------|
286 | | Basil Joy stuck on Bob shell flags | Pair with him immediately in first 4 hours |
287 | | SSE streaming breaks on deployment | Test Railway SSE before Hour 28 |
288 | | Providers not ready by Hour 14 | Use mock provider to keep integration moving |
289 | | Johann's frontend can't connect | Check CORS settings first |
290 | 
291 | ---
292 | 
293 | ## Submission Text (Fill This In)
294 | 
295 | **Title:** Elith
296 | 
297 | **Short Description (255 chars):**
298 | > Elith gives every AI model — Claude, Gemini, GPT, local LLMs — the same repo-awareness IBM Bob has natively, through an agent skill layer. Any model. Bob-level. Your choice.
299 | 
300 | **Long Description:**
301 | > Elith is a universal repo-aware agent framework built around IBM Bob's capabilities. IBM Bob is powerful because it lives inside your repository — reading files, writing code, running tests natively. But most developers also use Claude, Gemini, GPT, or local LLMs, which are repo-blind by default. Elith fixes this with a skill layer of 12 agent tools — read_file, write_file, search_code, git_diff, run_tests, and more — injected into any model via tool calling. Every model gets Bob-level repo awareness. Beyond model parity, Elith's context engine loads only task-relevant files (typically 6 of 300+) from the repo and Obsidian-style project memory — eliminating token waste. The novel architecture generator analyzes your actual codebase patterns and proposes non-textbook solutions tailored to your constraints. Bob sets the standard. Elith brings everyone up to it.
302 | 
303 | ---
304 | 
305 | *Your Task Doc — Elith — IBM Bob Hackathon 2026*

<file><path>docs/my_part/CODE_TEMPLATES.md</path>
<content lines="1-533">
  1 | # Elith Backend - Code Templates
  2 | **Quick reference for implementation**
  3 | 
  4 | ---
  5 | 
  6 | ## 1. Project Setup Files
  7 | 
  8 | ### pyproject.toml
  9 | ```toml
 10 | [project]
 11 | name = "elith"
 12 | version = "0.1.0"
 13 | description = "Universal repo-aware agent framework"
 14 | requires-python = ">=3.10"
 15 | dependencies = [
 16 |     "fastapi>=0.104.0",
 17 |     "uvicorn[standard]>=0.24.0",
 18 |     "pydantic>=2.5.0",
 19 |     "python-multipart>=0.0.6",
 20 |     "sse-starlette>=1.8.0",
 21 | ]
 22 | 
 23 | [build-system]
 24 | requires = ["setuptools>=68.0"]
 25 | build-backend = "setuptools.build_meta"
 26 | ```
 27 | 
 28 | ### .env.example
 29 | ```bash
 30 | # API Keys
 31 | ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=your_claude_key_here
 32 | GOOGLE_API_KEY=your_gemini_key_here
 33 | OPENAI_API_KEY=your_openai_key_here
 34 | 
 35 | # Server
 36 | HOST=0.0.0.0
 37 | PORT=8000
 38 | ```
 39 | 
 40 | ### .gitignore
 41 | ```
 42 | __pycache__/
 43 | *.py[cod]
 44 | .Python
 45 | venv/
 46 | .env
 47 | .vscode/
 48 | .DS_Store
 49 | bob-reports/*.md
 50 | !bob-reports/.gitkeep
 51 | ```
 52 | 
 53 | ---
 54 | 
 55 | ## 2. Pydantic Models
 56 | 
 57 | ### backend/models/task_packet.py
 58 | ```python
 59 | from pydantic import BaseModel
 60 | from typing import List, Optional
 61 | 
 62 | class FileInfo(BaseModel):
 63 |     path: str
 64 |     size: int
 65 |     extension: str
 66 |     is_key_file: bool = False
 67 | 
 68 | class Note(BaseModel):
 69 |     filename: str
 70 |     content: str
 71 |     tags: List[str] = []
 72 | 
 73 | class TaskPacket(BaseModel):
 74 |     repo_path: str
 75 |     vault_path: Optional[str] = None
 76 |     files: List[FileInfo]
 77 |     vault_notes: List[Note]
 78 |     total_files: int
 79 |     selected_files: List[str] = []
 80 | ```
 81 | 
 82 | ### backend/models/session.py
 83 | ```python
 84 | from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
 85 | from typing import List, Optional
 86 | from enum import Enum
 87 | from datetime import datetime
 88 | 
 89 | class SessionStatus(str, Enum):
 90 |     PENDING = "pending"
 91 |     RUNNING = "running"
 92 |     COMPLETED = "completed"
 93 |     ERROR = "error"
 94 | 
 95 | class Session(BaseModel):
 96 |     session_id: str
 97 |     model: str
 98 |     operation: str
 99 |     repo_path: str
100 |     vault_path: Optional[str] = None
101 |     status: SessionStatus = SessionStatus.PENDING
102 |     output: str = ""
103 |     files_changed: List[str] = []
104 |     report_path: Optional[str] = None
105 |     created_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=datetime.utcnow)
106 |     completed_at: Optional[datetime] = None
107 | ```
108 | 
109 | ---
110 | 
111 | ## 3. Context Engine
112 | 
113 | ### backend/context_engine/repo_scanner.py
114 | ```python
115 | from pathlib import Path
116 | from typing import List, Set
117 | from ..models.task_packet import FileInfo
118 | 
119 | class RepoScanner:
120 |     SKIP_DIRS: Set[str] = {
121 |         '.git', 'node_modules', '__pycache__', 'venv', '.venv',
122 |         'env', 'dist', 'build', '.next', 'target'
123 |     }
124 |     
125 |     KEY_FILES: Set[str] = {
126 |         'README.md', 'package.json', 'pyproject.toml',
127 |         'main.py', 'app.py', 'index.js'
128 |     }
129 |     
130 |     def __init__(self, repo_path: str):
131 |         self.repo_path = Path(repo_path).resolve()
132 |         if not self.repo_path.exists():
133 |             raise ValueError(f"Path does not exist: {repo_path}")
134 |     
135 |     def scan(self) -> List[FileInfo]:
136 |         files = []
137 |         for file_path in self._walk_directory(self.repo_path):
138 |             relative_path = str(file_path.relative_to(self.repo_path))
139 |             try:
140 |                 files.append(FileInfo(
141 |                     path=relative_path,
142 |                     size=file_path.stat().st_size,
143 |                     extension=file_path.suffix,
144 |                     is_key_file=file_path.name in self.KEY_FILES
145 |                 ))
146 |             except (OSError, PermissionError):
147 |                 continue
148 |         return files
149 |     
150 |     def _walk_directory(self, directory: Path):
151 |         try:
152 |             for item in directory.iterdir():
153 |                 if item.is_dir() and item.name not in self.SKIP_DIRS:
154 |                     yield from self._walk_directory(item)
155 |                 elif item.is_file():
156 |                     yield item
157 |         except PermissionError:
158 |             pass
159 | ```
160 | 
161 | ### backend/context_engine/vault_reader.py
162 | ```python
163 | from pathlib import Path
164 | from typing import List, Optional
165 | import re
166 | from ..models.task_packet import Note
167 | 
168 | class VaultReader:
169 |     def __init__(self, vault_path: Optional[str] = None):
170 |         self.vault_path = Path(vault_path).resolve() if vault_path else None
171 |     
172 |     def read_notes(self) -> List[Note]:
173 |         if not self.vault_path or not self.vault_path.exists():
174 |             return []
175 |         
176 |         notes = []
177 |         for md_file in self.vault_path.rglob("*.md"):
178 |             try:
179 |                 content = md_file.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
180 |                 notes.append(Note(
181 |                     filename=md_file.name,
182 |                     content=content,
183 |                     tags=self._extract_tags(content)
184 |                 ))
185 |             except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
186 |                 continue
187 |         return notes
188 |     
189 |     def _extract_tags(self, content: str) -> List[str]:
190 |         return list(set(re.findall(r'(?<!\#)\#([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)', content)))
191 | ```
192 | 
193 | ### backend/context_engine/packet_builder.py
194 | ```python
195 | from typing import List, Dict
196 | from pathlib import Path
197 | from ..models.task_packet import TaskPacket, Note
198 | 
199 | class PacketBuilder:
200 |     TASK_PATTERNS = {
201 |         "explain": {"max_files": 6},
202 |         "architect": {"max_files": 6},
203 |         "test-gen": {"max_files": 4},
204 |         "refactor": {"max_files": 4}
205 |     }
206 |     
207 |     def __init__(self, task_packet: TaskPacket):
208 |         self.packet = task_packet
209 |     
210 |     def build_context(self, operation: str, target_file: str = None) -> Dict:
211 |         pattern = self.TASK_PATTERNS.get(operation, {"max_files": 4})
212 |         
213 |         # Start with key files
214 |         selected = [f.path for f in self.packet.files if f.is_key_file]
215 |         
216 |         # Add operation-specific files
217 |         if operation == "test-gen":
218 |             selected.extend(self._find_files_without_tests())
219 |         elif operation in ["explain", "architect"]:
220 |             selected.extend(self._find_entry_points())
221 |         
222 |         # Limit to max
223 |         selected = selected[:pattern["max_files"]]
224 |         
225 |         return {
226 |             "selected_files": selected,
227 |             "context": self._build_context_string(selected),
228 |             "vault_notes": self._find_relevant_notes(operation),
229 |             "total_files": self.packet.total_files,
230 |             "files_loaded": len(selected)
231 |         }
232 |     
233 |     def _build_context_string(self, file_paths: List[str]) -> str:
234 |         repo_path = Path(self.packet.repo_path)
235 |         parts = [
236 |             f"Repository: {self.packet.repo_path}",
237 |             f"Total files: {self.packet.total_files}",
238 |             f"Files loaded: {len(file_paths)}\n"
239 |         ]
240 |         
241 |         for path in file_paths:
242 |             try:
243 |                 content = (repo_path / path).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
244 |                 parts.append(f"\n=== {path} ===\n{content}\n")
245 |             except:
246 |                 parts.append(f"\n=== {path} ===\n[Unreadable]\n")
247 |         
248 |         return "\n".join(parts)
249 |     
250 |     def _find_files_without_tests(self) -> List[str]:
251 |         # Simplified: return first few non-test files
252 |         return [f.path for f in self.packet.files 
253 |                 if 'test' not in f.path.lower() 
254 |                 and f.extension in ['.py', '.js', '.ts']][:3]
255 |     
256 |     def _find_entry_points(self) -> List[str]:
257 |         entry_names = ['main.py', 'app.py', 'index.js', 'main.go']
258 |         return [f.path for f in self.packet.files 
259 |                 if Path(f.path).name in entry_names][:2]
260 |     
261 |     def _find_relevant_notes(self, operation: str) -> List[Note]:
262 |         keywords = {
263 |             "architect": ["architecture", "design"],
264 |             "test-gen": ["testing", "test"],
265 |             "explain": ["overview", "architecture"]
266 |         }
267 |         terms = keywords.get(operation, [])
268 |         return [n for n in self.packet.vault_notes 
269 |                 if any(t in n.content.lower() for t in terms)][:2]
270 | ```
271 | 
272 | ---
273 | 
274 | ## 4. FastAPI Routes
275 | 
276 | ### backend/main.py
277 | ```python
278 | from fastapi import FastAPI
279 | from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
280 | from .routes import scan, execute, stream, models, results, tasks
281 | 
282 | app = FastAPI(title="Elith API", version="0.1.0")
283 | 
284 | app.add_middleware(
285 |     CORSMiddleware,
286 |     allow_origins=["*"],
287 |     allow_credentials=True,
288 |     allow_methods=["*"],
289 |     allow_headers=["*"],
290 | )
291 | 
292 | app.include_router(scan.router, prefix="/api", tags=["scan"])
293 | app.include_router(execute.router, prefix="/api", tags=["execute"])
294 | app.include_router(stream.router, prefix="/api", tags=["stream"])
295 | app.include_router(models.router, prefix="/api", tags=["models"])
296 | app.include_router(results.router, prefix="/api", tags=["results"])
297 | app.include_router(tasks.router, prefix="/api", tags=["tasks"])
298 | 
299 | @app.get("/")
300 | async def root():
301 |     return {"name": "Elith API", "version": "0.1.0", "status": "running"}
302 | 
303 | @app.get("/health")
304 | async def health():
305 |     return {"status": "healthy"}
306 | ```
307 | 
308 | ### backend/routes/scan.py
309 | ```python
310 | from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
311 | from pydantic import BaseModel
312 | from typing import Optional
313 | from ..context_engine.repo_scanner import RepoScanner
314 | from ..context_engine.vault_reader import VaultReader
315 | from ..models.task_packet import TaskPacket
316 | 
317 | router = APIRouter()
318 | 
319 | class ScanRequest(BaseModel):
320 |     repo_path: str
321 |     vault_path: Optional[str] = None
322 | 
323 | @router.post("/scan")
324 | async def scan_repository(request: ScanRequest) -> TaskPacket:
325 |     try:
326 |         scanner = RepoScanner(request.repo_path)
327 |         files = scanner.scan()
328 |         
329 |         vault_reader = VaultReader(request.vault_path)
330 |         notes = vault_reader.read_notes()
331 |         
332 |         return TaskPacket(
333 |             repo_path=request.repo_path,
334 |             vault_path=request.vault_path,
335 |             files=files,
336 |             vault_notes=notes,
337 |             total_files=len(files)
338 |         )
339 |     except ValueError as e:
340 |         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e))
341 |     except Exception as e:
342 |         raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Scan failed: {str(e)}")
343 | ```
344 | 
345 | ---
346 | 
347 | ## 5. Session Management
348 | 
349 | ### backend/session/manager.py
350 | ```python
351 | from typing import Dict, Optional
352 | import asyncio
353 | from ..models.session import Session, SessionStatus
354 | 
355 | class SessionManager:
356 |     def __init__(self):
357 |         self.sessions: Dict[str, Session] = {}
358 |         self.output_queues: Dict[str, asyncio.Queue] = {}
359 |     
360 |     def create_session(self, session_id: str, model: str, 
361 |                       operation: str, repo_path: str) -> Session:
362 |         session = Session(
363 |             session_id=session_id,
364 |             model=model,
365 |             operation=operation,
366 |             repo_path=repo_path
367 |         )
368 |         self.sessions[session_id] = session
369 |         self.output_queues[session_id] = asyncio.Queue()
370 |         return session
371 |     
372 |     def get_session(self, session_id: str) -> Optional[Session]:
373 |         return self.sessions.get(session_id)
374 |     
375 |     async def add_output(self, session_id: str, content: str):
376 |         if session_id in self.sessions:
377 |             self.sessions[session_id].output += content
378 |             if session_id in self.output_queues:
379 |                 await self.output_queues[session_id].put(content)
380 |     
381 |     async def stream_output(self, session_id: str):
382 |         if session_id not in self.output_queues:
383 |             return
384 |         
385 |         queue = self.output_queues[session_id]
386 |         while True:
387 |             try:
388 |                 content = await asyncio.wait_for(queue.get(), timeout=0.1)
389 |                 yield content
390 |             except asyncio.TimeoutError:
391 |                 session = self.sessions.get(session_id)
392 |                 if session and session.status in [SessionStatus.COMPLETED, SessionStatus.ERROR]:
393 |                     break
394 | 
395 | manager = SessionManager()
396 | ```
397 | 
398 | ### backend/session/logger.py
399 | ```python
400 | from pathlib import Path
401 | from ..models.session import Session
402 | 
403 | class SessionLogger:
404 |     def __init__(self, reports_dir: str = "bob-reports"):
405 |         self.reports_dir = Path(reports_dir)
406 |         self.reports_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
407 |         (self.reports_dir / ".gitkeep").touch()
408 |     
409 |     def log_session(self, session: Session) -> str:
410 |         timestamp = session.created_at.strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
411 |         filename = f"session_{timestamp}_{session.session_id[:8]}.md"
412 |         filepath = self.reports_dir / filename
413 |         
414 |         content = f"""# Elith Session Report
415 | 
416 | **Session ID:** {session.session_id}
417 | **Model:** {session.model}
418 | **Operation:** {session.operation}
419 | **Status:** {session.status.value}
420 | 
421 | ## Repository
422 | {session.repo_path}
423 | 
424 | ## Output
425 | ```
426 | {session.output}
427 | ```
428 | 
429 | ---
430 | *Generated by Elith*
431 | """
432 |         filepath.write_text(content, encoding='utf-8')
433 |         return str(filepath)
434 | 
435 | logger = SessionLogger()
436 | ```
437 | 
438 | ---
439 | 
440 | ## 6. Model Router
441 | 
442 | ### backend/router/model_router.py
443 | ```python
444 | from typing import Generator
445 | from ..providers.base_provider import BaseProvider
446 | 
447 | class ModelRouter:
448 |     def __init__(self):
449 |         self.providers = {}
450 |     
451 |     def register_provider(self, name: str, provider: BaseProvider):
452 |         self.providers[name] = provider
453 |     
454 |     def route(self, model: str, prompt: str, context: str) -> Generator[str, None, None]:
455 |         if model not in self.providers:
456 |             yield f"Error: Model '{model}' not configured"
457 |             return
458 |         
459 |         provider = self.providers[model]
460 |         yield from provider.run(prompt, context)
461 |     
462 |     def get_available_models(self) -> list:
463 |         return list(self.providers.keys())
464 | 
465 | router = ModelRouter()
466 | ```
467 | 
468 | ---
469 | 
470 | ## 7. Operations
471 | 
472 | ### backend/operations/explain.py
473 | ```python
474 | def build_explain_prompt(context: str) -> str:
475 |     return f"""Analyze this codebase and explain its architecture.
476 | 
477 | {context}
478 | 
479 | Provide:
480 | 1. Overall structure
481 | 2. Key components
482 | 3. How they interact
483 | 4. Technology stack
484 | 5. Entry points
485 | 
486 | Focus on helping a new developer understand quickly."""
487 | ```
488 | 
489 | ### backend/operations/architect.py
490 | ```python
491 | def build_architect_prompt(context: str) -> str:
492 |     return f"""Analyze this codebase and propose architectural improvements.
493 | 
494 | {context}
495 | 
496 | CRITICAL: Analyze THIS specific codebase. No generic answers.
497 | 
498 | Propose 2-3 improvements that are:
499 | - Tailored to THIS code's patterns
500 | - Based on actual files you see
501 | - Production-ready with migration paths
502 | 
503 | For each:
504 | - Why it fits THIS codebase (reference actual files)
505 | - Tradeoffs
506 | - Migration path
507 | - Risks and mitigation"""
508 | ```
509 | 
510 | ---
511 | 
512 | ## 8. Run Commands
513 | 
514 | ```bash
515 | # Install dependencies
516 | pip install -r requirements.txt
517 | 
518 | # Run server
519 | cd backend
520 | uvicorn main:app --reload --port 8000
521 | 
522 | # Test scan endpoint
523 | curl -X POST http://localhost:8000/api/scan \
524 |   -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
525 |   -d '{"repo_path": "/path/to/repo"}'
526 | 
527 | # Test health
528 | curl http://localhost:8000/health
529 | ```
530 | 
531 | ---
532 | 
533 | *Use these templates as starting points. Adapt as needed.*
</content>

<content>
"""Task packet models for context engine."""
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import List, Optional


class FileInfo(BaseModel):
    """Information about a file in the repository."""
    path: str
    size: int
    extension: str
    is_key_file: bool = False


class Note(BaseModel):
    """Obsidian vault note."""
    filename: str
    content: str
    tags: List[str] = []


class TaskPacket(BaseModel):
    """Complete context packet for a task."""
    repo_path: str
    vault_path: Optional[str] = None
    files: List[FileInfo]
    vault_notes: List[Note]
    total_files: int
    selected_files: List[str] = []
</content>
<line_count>28</line_count>
</write_to_file>

<path>backend/models/session.py</path>
<content>
"""Session models for tracking execution state."""
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from typing import List, Optional
from enum import Enum
from datetime import datetime


class SessionStatus(str, Enum):
    """Status of a session."""
    PENDING = "pending"
    RUNNING = "running"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    ERROR = "error"


class Session(BaseModel):
    """Execution session tracking."""
    session_id: str
    model: str
    operation: str
    repo_path: str
    vault_path: Optional[str] = None
    status: SessionStatus = SessionStatus.PENDING
    output: str = ""
    files_changed: List[str] = []
    report_path: Optional[str] = None
    created_at: datetime = Field(default_factory=datetime.utcnow)
    completed_at: Optional[datetime] = None
</content>
<line_count>29</line_count>
</write_to_file>

<content>
"""Repository scanner for file discovery."""
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Set
from ..models.task_packet import FileInfo


class RepoScanner:
    """Scans repository and identifies files."""
    
    SKIP_DIRS: Set[str] = {
        '.git', 'node_modules', '__pycache__', 'venv', '.venv',
        'env', 'dist', 'build', '.next', 'target', '.pytest_cache',
        '.mypy_cache', 'coverage', '.coverage', 'htmlcov'
    }
    
    KEY_FILES: Set[str] = {
        'README.md', 'package.json', 'pyproject.toml', 'setup.py',
        'main.py', 'app.py', 'index.js', 'index.ts', 'main.go',
        'Cargo.toml', 'go.mod', 'requirements.txt'
    }
    
    def __init__(self, repo_path: str):
        """Initialize scanner with repository path."""
        self.repo_path = Path(repo_path).resolve()
        if not self.repo_path.exists():
            raise ValueError(f"Path does not exist: {repo_path}")
        if not self.repo_path.is_dir():
            raise ValueError(f"Path is not a directory: {repo_path}")
    
    def scan(self) -> List[FileInfo]:
        """Scan repository and return file information."""
        files = []
        for file_path in self._walk_directory(self.repo_path):
            relative_path = str(file_path.relative_to(self.repo_path))
            try:
                files.append(FileInfo(
                    path=relative_path,
                    size=file_path.stat().st_size,
                    extension=file_path.suffix,
                    is_key_file=file_path.name in self.KEY_FILES
                ))
            except (OSError, PermissionError):
                continue
        return files
    
    def _walk_directory(self, directory: Path):
        """Recursively walk directory, skipping ignored paths."""
        try:
            for item in directory.iterdir():
                if item.is_dir() and item.name not in self.SKIP_DIRS:
                    yield from self._walk_directory(item)
                elif item.is_file():
                    yield item
        except PermissionError:
            pass
</content>

<content>
"""Obsidian vault reader for project memory."""
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Optional
import re
from ..models.task_packet import Note


class VaultReader:
    """Reads markdown notes from Obsidian vault."""
    
    def __init__(self, vault_path: Optional[str] = None):
        """Initialize vault reader with optional vault path."""
        self.vault_path = Path(vault_path).resolve() if vault_path else None
    
    def read_notes(self) -> List[Note]:
        """Read all markdown notes from vault."""
        if not self.vault_path or not self.vault_path.exists():
            return []
        
        if not self.vault_path.is_dir():
            return []
        
        notes = []
        for md_file in self.vault_path.rglob("*.md"):
            try:
                content = md_file.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
                notes.append(Note(
                    filename=md_file.name,
                    content=content,
                    tags=self._extract_tags(content)
                ))
            except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
                continue
        return notes
    
    def _extract_tags(self, content: str) -> List[str]:
        """Extract hashtags from markdown content."""
        # Match #tag but not ##heading
        tags = re.findall(r'(?<!\#)\#([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)', content)
        return list(set(tags))
</content>

<content>
"""Context packet builder for smart file selection."""
from typing import List, Dict
from pathlib import Path
from ..models.task_packet import TaskPacket, Note


class PacketBuilder:
    """Builds minimal context packets for tasks."""
    
    TASK_PATTERNS = {
        "explain": {"max_files": 6},
        "architect": {"max_files": 6},
        "test-gen": {"max_files": 4},
        "refactor": {"max_files": 4}
    }
    
    def __init__(self, task_packet: TaskPacket):
        """Initialize with scanned task packet."""
        self.packet = task_packet
    
    def build_context(self, operation: str, target_file: str = None) -> Dict:
        """Build minimal context for operation."""
        pattern = self.TASK_PATTERNS.get(operation, {"max_files": 4})
        
        # Start with key files
        selected = [f.path for f in self.packet.files if f.is_key_file]
        
        # Add operation-specific files
        if operation == "test-gen":
            selected.extend(self._find_files_without_tests())
        elif operation in ["explain", "architect"]:
            selected.extend(self._find_entry_points())
        elif operation == "refactor" and target_file:
            selected.extend(self._find_related_files(target_file))
        
        # Limit to max
        selected = list(dict.fromkeys(selected))  # Remove duplicates
        selected = selected[:pattern["max_files"]]
        
        return {
            "selected_files": selected,
            "context": self._build_context_string(selected),
            "vault_notes": self._find_relevant_notes(operation),
            "total_files": self.packet.total_files,
            "files_loaded": len(selected)
        }
    
    def _build_context_string(self, file_paths: List[str]) -> str:
        """Build context string with file contents."""
        repo_path = Path(self.packet.repo_path)
        parts = [
            f"Repository: {self.packet.repo_path}",
            f"Total files: {self.packet.total_files}",
            f"Files loaded: {len(file_paths)}\n"
        ]
        
        for path in file_paths:
            try:
                full_path = repo_path / path
                content = full_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
                parts.append(f"\n=== {path} ===\n{content}\n")
            except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
                parts.append(f"\n=== {path} ===\n[Unreadable]\n")
        
        return "\n".join(parts)
    
    def _find_files_without_tests(self) -> List[str]:
        """Find source files without corresponding tests."""
        source_files = []
        test_files = set()
        
        for f in self.packet.files:
            if 'test' in f.path.lower() or 'spec' in f.path.lower():
                test_files.add(f.path)
            elif f.extension in ['.py', '.js', '.ts', '.go', '.rs']:
                source_files.append(f.path)
        
        # Return source files that don't have tests
        return [f for f in source_files if not self._has_test_file(f, test_files)][:3]
    
    def _has_test_file(self, source_path: str, test_files: set) -> bool:
        """Check if source file has a corresponding test."""
        base_name = Path(source_path).stem
        return any(base_name in test_file for test_file in test_files)
    
    def _find_entry_points(self) -> List[str]:
        """Find main entry point files."""
        entry_names = ['main.py', 'app.py', 'index.js', 'index.ts', 'main.go', 'main.rs']
        return [f.path for f in self.packet.files 
                if Path(f.path).name in entry_names][:2]
    
    def _find_related_files(self, target_file: str) -> List[str]:
        """Find files related to target file."""
        # Simple heuristic: files in same directory
        target_dir = str(Path(target_file).parent)
        related = [f.path for f in self.packet.files 
                   if str(Path(f.path).parent) == target_dir]
        return related[:3]
    
    def _find_relevant_notes(self, operation: str) -> List[Note]:
        """Find vault notes relevant to operation."""
        keywords = {
            "architect": ["architecture", "design", "pattern"],
            "test-gen": ["testing", "test", "quality"],
            "explain": ["overview", "architecture", "readme"],
            "refactor": ["refactor", "improve", "technical-debt"]
        }
        terms = keywords.get(operation, [])
        
        relevant = []
        for note in self.packet.vault_notes:
            content_lower = note.content.lower()
            if any(term in content_lower for term in terms):
                relevant.append(note)
        
        return relevant[:2]
</content>

<content>
"""Scan endpoint for repository and vault analysis."""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Optional
from ..context_engine.repo_scanner import RepoScanner
from ..context_engine.vault_reader import VaultReader
from ..models.task_packet import TaskPacket

router = APIRouter()


class ScanRequest(BaseModel):
    """Request model for scan endpoint."""
    repo_path: str
    vault_path: Optional[str] = None


@router.post("/scan")
async def scan_repository(request: ScanRequest) -> TaskPacket:
    """
    Scan repository and vault to build task packet.
    
    Returns file list, vault notes, and metadata.
    """
    try:
        scanner = RepoScanner(request.repo_path)
        files = scanner.scan()
        
        vault_reader = VaultReader(request.vault_path)
        notes = vault_reader.read_notes()
        
        return TaskPacket(
            repo_path=request.repo_path,
            vault_path=request.vault_path,
            files=files,
            vault_notes=notes,
            total_files=len(files)
        )
    except ValueError as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e))
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Scan failed: {str(e)}")
</content>

<path>backend/session/manager.py</path>
<content>
"""Session manager for tracking execution state."""
from typing import Dict, Optional
import asyncio
from ..models.session import Session, SessionStatus


class SessionManager:
    """Manages active sessions and output streaming."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        """Initialize session manager."""
        self.sessions: Dict[str, Session] = {}
        self.output_queues: Dict[str, asyncio.Queue] = {}
    
    def create_session(
        self, 
        session_id: str, 
        model: str, 
        operation: str, 
        repo_path: str,
        vault_path: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> Session:
        """Create a new session."""
        session = Session(
            session_id=session_id,
            model=model,
            operation=operation,
            repo_path=repo_path,
            vault_path=vault_path
        )
        self.sessions[session_id] = session
        self.output_queues[session_id] = asyncio.Queue()
        return session
    
    def get_session(self, session_id: str) -> Optional[Session]:
        """Get session by ID."""
        return self.sessions.get(session_id)
    
    async def add_output(self, session_id: str, content: str):
        """Add output to session and queue for streaming."""
        if session_id in self.sessions:
            self.sessions[session_id].output += content
            if session_id in self.output_queues:
                await self.output_queues[session_id].put(content)
    
    async def stream_output(self, session_id: str):
        """Stream output from session queue."""
        if session_id not in self.output_queues:
            return
        
        queue = self.output_queues[session_id]
        while True:
            try:
                content = await asyncio.wait_for(queue.get(), timeout=0.1)
                yield content
            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                session = self.sessions.get(session_id)
                if session and session.status in [SessionStatus.COMPLETED, SessionStatus.ERROR]:
                    break
    
    def update_status(self, session_id: str, status: SessionStatus):
        """Update session status."""
        if session_id in self.sessions:
            self.sessions[session_id].status = status
    
    def cleanup_session(self, session_id: str):
        """Clean up session resources."""
        if session_id in self.output_queues:
            del self.output_queues[session_id]


# Global session manager instance
manager = SessionManager()
</content>

<content>
"""Session logger for Bob reports."""
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
from ..models.session import Session


class SessionLogger:
    """Logs sessions to bob-reports directory."""
    
    def __init__(self, reports_dir: str = "bob-reports"):
        """Initialize logger with reports directory."""
        self.reports_dir = Path(reports_dir)
        self.reports_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        (self.reports_dir / ".gitkeep").touch()
    
    def log_session(self, session: Session) -> str:
        """
        Log session to markdown file.
        
        Returns path to created report file.
        """
        timestamp = session.created_at.strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
        filename = f"session_{timestamp}_{session.session_id[:8]}.md"
        filepath = self.reports_dir / filename
        
        content = self._build_report(session)
        filepath.write_text(content, encoding='utf-8')
        
        return str(filepath)
    
    def _build_report(self, session: Session) -> str:
        """Build markdown report content."""
        lines = [
            "# Elith Session Report",
            "",
            f"**Session ID:** {session.session_id}",
            f"**Model:** {session.model}",
            f"**Operation:** {session.operation}",
            f"**Status:** {session.status.value}",
            f"**Created:** {session.created_at.isoformat()}",
        ]
        
        if session.completed_at:
            lines.append(f"**Completed:** {session.completed_at.isoformat()}")
        
        lines.extend([
            "",
            "## Repository",
            f"{session.repo_path}",
            ""
        ])
        
        if session.vault_path:
            lines.extend([
                "## Vault",
                f"{session.vault_path}",
                ""
            ])
        
        if session.files_changed:
            lines.extend([
                "## Files Changed",
                ""
            ])
            for file in session.files_changed:
                lines.append(f"- {file}")
            lines.append("")
        
        lines.extend([
            "## Output",
            "```",
            session.output,
            "```",
            "",
            "---",
            "*Generated by Elith*"
        ])
        
        return "\n".join(lines)


# Global logger instance
logger = SessionLogger()
</content>
<line_count>82</line_count>
</write_to_file>

"""Tasks endpoint for available operations."""
from fastapi import APIRouter
from typing import List, Dict

router = APIRouter()


@router.get("/tasks")
async def get_tasks() -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
    """
    Get list of available operations.
    
    Returns available task types that can be executed.
    """
    return {
        "operations": [
            "explain",
            "architect",
            "test-gen",
            "refactor"
        ]
    }
</content>

<content>
"""Results endpoint for session output."""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
from ..session.manager import manager

router = APIRouter()


@router.get("/results/{session_id}")
async def get_results(session_id: str) -> Dict:
    """
    Get results for a completed session.
    
    Returns output, files changed, and report path.
    """
    session = manager.get_session(session_id)
    
    if not session:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Session not found")
    
    return {
        "session_id": session.session_id,
        "model": session.model,
        "operation": session.operation,
        "status": session.status.value,
        "output": session.output,
        "files_changed": session.files_changed,
        "report_path": session.report_path,
        "created_at": session.created_at.isoformat(),
        "completed_at": session.completed_at.isoformat() if session.completed_at else None
    }
</content>

"""Execute endpoint for starting operations."""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, BackgroundTasks
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Optional
import uuid
from ..session.manager import manager
from ..session.logger import logger
from ..models.session import SessionStatus

router = APIRouter()


class ExecuteRequest(BaseModel):
    """Request model for execute endpoint."""
    model: str
    operation: str
    repo_path: str
    vault_path: Optional[str] = None


class ExecuteResponse(BaseModel):
    """Response model for execute endpoint."""
    session_id: str
    status: str


@router.post("/execute")
async def execute_operation(
    request: ExecuteRequest,
    background_tasks: BackgroundTasks
) -> ExecuteResponse:
    """
    Start an operation execution.
    
    Returns session_id for tracking progress via /stream endpoint.
    """
    # Generate session ID
    session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    
    # Create session
    session = manager.create_session(
        session_id=session_id,
        model=request.model,
        operation=request.operation,
        repo_path=request.repo_path,
        vault_path=request.vault_path
    )
    
    # TODO: Add background task to actually run the operation
    # For now, just mark as pending
    # background_tasks.add_task(run_operation, session_id, request)
    
    return ExecuteResponse(
        session_id=session_id,
        status=session.status.value
    )


async def run_operation(session_id: str, request: ExecuteRequest):
    """
    Background task to run the operation.
    
    This will be implemented when providers are ready.
    """
    try:
        manager.update_status(session_id, SessionStatus.RUNNING)
        
        # TODO: 
        # 1. Build context packet
        # 2. Route to appropriate provider
        # 3. Stream output to session
        # 4. Log session report
        
        await manager.add_output(session_id, "Operation started...\n")
        
        manager.update_status(session_id, SessionStatus.COMPLETED)
        
        # Log session
        session = manager.get_session(session_id)
        if session:
            report_path = logger.log_session(session)
            session.report_path = report_path
            
    except Exception as e:
        manager.update_status(session_id, SessionStatus.ERROR)
        await manager.add_output(session_id, f"Error: {str(e)}\n")
</content>

<content>
"""Stream endpoint for SSE output streaming."""
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from sse_starlette.sse import EventSourceResponse
from ..session.manager import manager
import json

router = APIRouter()


@router.get("/stream/{session_id}")
async def stream_output(session_id: str):
    """
    Stream session output via Server-Sent Events.
    
    Frontend connects to this endpoint to receive live output.
    """
    session = manager.get_session(session_id)
    
    if not session:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Session not found")
    
    async def event_generator():
        """Generate SSE events from session output."""
        async for content in manager.stream_output(session_id):
            yield {
                "event": "message",
                "data": json.dumps({
                    "type": "output",
                    "content": content,
                    "model": session.model
                })
            }
        
        # Send completion event
        yield {
            "event": "message",
            "data": json.dumps({
                "type": "done",
                "status": session.status.value,
                "model": session.model
            })
        }
    
    return EventSourceResponse(event_generator())
</content>

<content>
"""Elith FastAPI backend."""
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from .routes import scan, execute, stream, models, results, tasks

app = FastAPI(
    title="Elith API",
    version="0.1.0",
    description="Universal repo-aware agent framework"
)

# CORS middleware for frontend
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],  # TODO: Configure for production
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

# Include routers
app.include_router(scan.router, prefix="/api", tags=["scan"])
app.include_router(execute.router, prefix="/api", tags=["execute"])
app.include_router(stream.router, prefix="/api", tags=["stream"])
app.include_router(models.router, prefix="/api", tags=["models"])
app.include_router(results.router, prefix="/api", tags=["results"])
app.include_router(tasks.router, prefix="/api", tags=["tasks"])


@app.get("/")
async def root():
    """Root endpoint."""
    return {
        "name": "Elith API",
        "version": "0.1.0",
        "status": "running",
        "docs": "/docs"
    }


@app.get("/health")
async def health():
    """Health check endpoint."""
    return {"status": "healthy"}

<content>
"""Base provider interface for AI models."""
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import Generator


class BaseProvider(ABC):
    """Abstract base class for AI model providers."""
    
    def __init__(self, model_name: str):
        """Initialize provider with model name."""
        self.model_name = model_name
    
    @abstractmethod
    def run(self, prompt: str, context: str) -> Generator[str, None, None]:
        """
        Execute prompt with context and yield output chunks.
        
        Args:
            prompt: The operation prompt
            context: The context string with file contents
            
        Yields:
            Output chunks as they are generated
        """
        pass
    
    @abstractmethod
    def is_configured(self) -> bool:
        """Check if provider is properly configured."""
        pass
</content>

<content>
"""Model router for dispatching to providers."""
from typing import Generator, Dict
from ..providers.base_provider import BaseProvider


class ModelRouter:
    """Routes requests to appropriate AI model providers."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        """Initialize router with empty provider registry."""
        self.providers: Dict[str, BaseProvider] = {}
    
    def register_provider(self, name: str, provider: BaseProvider):
        """
        Register a provider.
        
        Args:
            name: Provider name (e.g., "bob", "claude")
            provider: Provider instance
        """
        self.providers[name] = provider
    
    def route(self, model: str, prompt: str, context: str) -> Generator[str, None, None]:
        """
        Route request to appropriate provider.
        
        Args:
            model: Model name to use
            prompt: Operation prompt
            context: Context string with file contents
            
        Yields:
            Output chunks from provider
        """
        if model not in self.providers:
            yield f"Error: Model '{model}' not configured\n"
            return
        
        provider = self.providers[model]
        
        if not provider.is_configured():
            yield f"Error: Model '{model}' is not properly configured\n"
            return
        
        yield from provider.run(prompt, context)
    
    def get_available_models(self) -> list:
        """Get list of registered model names."""
        return list(self.providers.keys())
    
    def get_configured_models(self) -> list:
        """Get list of properly configured models."""
        return [
            name for name, provider in self.providers.items()
            if provider.is_configured()
        ]


# Global router instance
router = ModelRouter()
</content>

</write_to_file>

"""Explain operation for codebase analysis."""


def build_explain_prompt(context: str) -> str:
    """
    Build prompt for explaining codebase architecture.
    
    Args:
        context: Context string with file contents
        
    Returns:
        Formatted prompt for the operation
    """
    return f"""Analyze this codebase and explain its architecture in clear, plain language.

{context}

Provide a comprehensive explanation covering:

1. **Overall Structure**: How is the codebase organized? What are the main directories/modules?

2. **Key Components**: What are the core components and what does each do?

3. **How They Interact**: How do the components communicate and work together?

4. **Technology Stack**: What languages, frameworks, and tools are being used?

5. **Entry Points**: Where does execution begin? What are the main entry points?

Focus on helping a new developer understand the codebase quickly. Be specific and reference actual files you see in the context."""
</content>

"""Architect operation for novel architecture proposals."""


def build_architect_prompt(context: str) -> str:
    """
    Build prompt for generating architecture proposals.
    
    This is THE KEY OPERATION - must produce repo-specific, non-generic proposals.
    
    Args:
        context: Context string with file contents
        
    Returns:
        Formatted prompt for the operation
    """
    return f"""Analyze this codebase and propose architectural improvements.

{context}

**CRITICAL REQUIREMENTS:**

1. **Analyze THIS specific codebase** - No generic textbook answers
2. **Reference actual files** you see in the context above
3. **Base proposals on actual patterns** present in this code
4. **Tailor to THIS project's constraints** and existing architecture

**Your Task:**

Propose 2-3 architectural improvements that are:

- **Specific to this codebase**: Reference actual files, patterns, and structures you observe
- **Production-ready**: Include concrete implementation steps
- **Justified**: Explain why this fits THIS codebase (not just "best practice")

**For Each Proposal:**

1. **What to Change**: Specific files/modules affected (reference actual paths)
2. **Why This Fits**: How it aligns with existing patterns in THIS codebase
3. **Tradeoffs**: What you gain vs what you lose
4. **Migration Path**: Step-by-step implementation plan
5. **Risks**: What could go wrong and how to mitigate

**Example of GOOD (specific) vs BAD (generic):**

❌ BAD: "Use microservices for better scalability"
✅ GOOD: "Split auth.py (lines 150-300) into separate service because it's already isolated, has its own database tables (users, sessions), and the current monolith is hitting memory limits at 2GB"

Focus on improvements that make sense for THIS specific project, not universal best practices."""
</content>
<line_count>48</line_count>
</write_to_file>

"""Test generation operation."""


def build_test_gen_prompt(context: str) -> str:
    """
    Build prompt for generating tests.
    
    Args:
        context: Context string with file contents
        
    Returns:
        Formatted prompt for the operation
    """
    return f"""Generate tests for the source files in this codebase.

{context}

**Your Task:**

Generate comprehensive tests for the source files shown above. Follow these guidelines:

1. **Match Existing Style**: If tests already exist in the codebase, match their style and framework
2. **Test Framework**: Use the appropriate framework for the language:
   - Python: pytest or unittest
   - JavaScript/TypeScript: Jest or Mocha
   - Go: standard testing package
   - Rust: built-in test framework

3. **Coverage**: For each source file, generate tests that cover:
   - Happy path scenarios
   - Edge cases
   - Error handling
   - Boundary conditions

4. **Test Structure**: 
   - Clear test names that describe what's being tested
   - Arrange-Act-Assert pattern
   - Proper setup and teardown if needed
   - Mock external dependencies

5. **File Organization**: 
   - Place tests in appropriate test directories
   - Follow naming conventions (e.g., `test_*.py`, `*.test.js`)

**Output Format:**

For each source file, provide:
- Test file path
- Complete test code
- Brief explanation of what's being tested

Focus on practical, maintainable tests that would actually be useful in this codebase."""
</content>

Backend foundation complete - 20 individual commits created following GitHub strategy.

**Files Created:**

**Configuration:**
- [`pyproject.toml`](pyproject.toml) - Project dependencies
- [`requirements.txt`](requirements.txt) - Pip dependencies

**Models:**
- [`backend/models/task_packet.py`](backend/models/task_packet.py) - FileInfo, Note, TaskPacket
- [`backend/models/session.py`](backend/models/session.py) - Session, SessionStatus

**Context Engine:**
- [`backend/context_engine/repo_scanner.py`](backend/context_engine/repo_scanner.py) - Repository file discovery
- [`backend/context_engine/vault_reader.py`](backend/context_engine/vault_reader.py) - Obsidian vault reader
- [`backend/context_engine/packet_builder.py`](backend/context_engine/packet_builder.py) - Smart file selection (4-6 files max)

**Session Management:**
- [`backend/session/manager.py`](backend/session/manager.py) - Session tracking and output streaming
- [`backend/session/logger.py`](backend/session/logger.py) - Bob reports export

**API Routes:**
- [`backend/routes/scan.py`](backend/routes/scan.py) - POST /api/scan
- [`backend/routes/execute.py`](backend/routes/execute.py) - POST /api/execute
- [`backend/routes/stream.py`](backend/routes/stream.py) - GET /api/stream/{session_id}
- [`backend/routes/models.py`](backend/routes/models.py) - GET /api/models
- [`backend/routes/results.py`](backend/routes/results.py) - GET /api/results/{session_id}
- [`backend/routes/tasks.py`](backend/routes/tasks.py) - GET /api/tasks

**Core Integration:**
- [`backend/main.py`](backend/main.py) - FastAPI app with CORS and all routes
- [`backend/router/model_router.py`](backend/router/model_router.py) - Provider dispatch skeleton
- [`backend/providers/base_provider.py`](backend/providers/base_provider.py) - Provider interface

**Operations:**
- [`backend/operations/explain.py`](backend/operations/explain.py) - Codebase explanation
- [`backend/operations/architect.py`](backend/operations/architect.py) - Novel architecture proposals
- [`backend/operations/test_gen.py`](backend/operations/test_gen.py) - Test generation

**Ready for:**
- Basil Joy to integrate providers (bob, claude, gemini, openai, ollama)
- Johann to connect TUI and React frontend
- Testing with: `uvicorn backend.main:app --reload`

All commits follow proper format per [`docs/GITHUB_STRATEGY.md`](docs/GITHUB_STRATEGY.md).

async def run_operation(session_id: str, request: ExecuteRequest):
    """
    Background task to run the operation.
    
    Builds context packet, routes to provider, streams output.
    """
    try:
        manager.update_status(session_id, SessionStatus.RUNNING)
        await manager.add_output(session_id, f"Starting {request.operation} operation...\n")
        
        # 1. Scan repository and vault
        await manager.add_output(session_id, "Scanning repository...\n")
        scanner = RepoScanner(request.repo_path)
        files = scanner.scan()
        
        vault_reader = VaultReader(request.vault_path)
        notes = vault_reader.read_notes()
        
        # 2. Build task packet
        task_packet = TaskPacket(
            repo_path=request.repo_path,
            vault_path=request.vault_path,
            files=files,
            vault_notes=notes,
            total_files=len(files)
        )
        
        # 3. Build minimal context for operation
        await manager.add_output(session_id, f"Building context (loading {len(files)} files)...\n")
        packet_builder = PacketBuilder(task_packet)
        context_data = packet_builder.build_context(request.operation)
        
        await manager.add_output(
            session_id, 
            f"Context ready: {context_data['files_loaded']} of {context_data['total_files']} files selected\n"
        )
        
        # 4. Get operation prompt
        operation_map = {
            "explain": explain.build_explain_prompt,
            "architect": architect.build_architect_prompt,
            "test-gen": test_gen.build_test_gen_prompt,
        }
        
        if request.operation not in operation_map:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown operation: {request.operation}")
        
        prompt = operation_map[request.operation](context_data['context'])
        
        # 5. Route to provider and stream output
        await manager.add_output(session_id, f"Executing with {request.model}...\n\n")
        
        for chunk in model_router.route(request.model, prompt, context_data['context']):
            await manager.add_output(session_id, chunk)
        
        # 6. Mark complete and log
        manager.update_status(session_id, SessionStatus.COMPLETED)
        session = manager.get_session(session_id)
        if session:
            session.completed_at = datetime.utcnow()
            report_path = logger.log_session(session)
            session.report_path = report_path
            await manager.add_output(session_id, f"\n\nSession report: {report_path}\n")
            
    except Exception as e:
        manager.update_status(session_id, SessionStatus.ERROR)
        await manager.add_output(session_id, f"\n\nError: {str(e)}\n")

<content>
"""Refactor operation for code improvements."""


def build_refactor_prompt(context: str, target_file: str = None) -> str:
    """
    Build prompt for refactoring code.
    
    Args:
        context: Context string with file contents
        target_file: Optional specific file to refactor
        
    Returns:
        Formatted prompt for the operation
    """
    target_instruction = f"\n\nFocus specifically on refactoring: {target_file}" if target_file else ""
    
    return f"""Refactor the code in this codebase to improve quality, maintainability, and performance.

{context}{target_instruction}

**Your Task:**

Analyze the code and propose specific refactoring improvements. Focus on:

1. **Code Smells**: Identify and fix:
   - Long methods/functions (>50 lines)
   - Duplicate code
   - Complex conditionals
   - Magic numbers/strings
   - Poor naming

2. **Design Patterns**: Apply appropriate patterns where beneficial:
   - Extract reusable components
   - Separate concerns
   - Reduce coupling
   - Improve cohesion

3. **Performance**: Optimize where needed:
   - Inefficient algorithms
   - Unnecessary computations
   - Memory leaks
   - Resource management

4. **Maintainability**: Improve code clarity:
   - Better naming
   - Clear structure
   - Reduced complexity
   - Better error handling

**For Each Refactoring:**

1. **What to Change**: Specific code location (file, line numbers)
2. **Why**: What problem does this solve?
3. **How**: Show before/after code
4. **Impact**: What improves? Any risks?
5. **Testing**: How to verify the refactoring works?

**Output Format:**

For each refactoring, provide:
- File path and line numbers
- Current code (before)
- Refactored code (after)
- Explanation of improvement
- Test recommendations

Focus on practical, safe refactorings that provide clear value."""
</content>
